## Standing rules / decisions

- **2026-07-20 ~19:26Z (owner, IN FORCE, autonomous session): FULL AUTONOMY until the end of the session** — do not ask about anything; resolve forks yourself as best and as professionally as possible; ACCUMULATE questions (lab-emit queue + final packet after the session); only BLOCKER is parked. Also then: **GSL-112 spec APPROVED by the owner** (q-009 answered by owner, impl allowed), q-008 closed by default (tabs). Each turn still ends with a text report to the owner.
- **2026-07-23 ~13:40Z (owner, IN FORCE): ACCEPTANCE REPORT FORMAT** — professional HTML dashboard (English + Russian) at path `docs/reports/verifications/<TASK-KEY>/runs/NN/`; the report leads the owner ("what to pay attention to"); runs are iterative: run 01 → owner's error review → run 02 and so on. "Screenshots in the branch" as a delivery format — inadmissible; everything must be merged and available locally from main. **AMENDED ~18:30Z (owner, run 01 review): (a) for design work the report must contain the DESIGN itself — a separate `design/` folder inside the run with an HTML overview (snapshot of live pages + design system + screen cards); (b) "canvas" screenshots (fullPage across many screens) are FORBIDDEN — only compact viewport frames; redo run 01 gallery in runs/02.**

## Known-automation-traps (this machine)

- **chrome-devtools MCP fill() vs React controlled inputs:** fill(uid,value) sets DOM value but React state stays stale (applied=None) — use keystroke-level input (CDP Input.dispatchKeyEvent / insertText) for React forms. Proven run01→run02 adjudication 2026-08-16.

- **Spawn cap:** 4th simultaneous worker pane fails (`fork failed: Device not configured`). Max 3; queue.
- Window 1:0 — ours (%36). tmux sessions 0/2/3/4 belong to others.
- Transient `API Error: Server error mid-response` → one continue-nudge on the findings file (verified 2×).
- Session-limit hit is survived: workers quiesce, files intact, after reset continue on nudge (verified).

- **⚠️ CRITICAL RULE ADDITIONS (impl-lab-1 discoveries, for all future cycles):**
  1. **Hook shim mandatory:** any bash hook behind `tools/hooks/lab-log-hook.sh` (exit 0 always). Reason: (a) `exec python3 - <<'PY'` — heredoc goes to stdin, hook silently writes nothing (placebo, exit 0); (b) exit 2 in a bash hook = "block operation" per Claude Code protocol, and a bash syntax error also gives 2 — an unfinished script blocked every Write/Edit in the live session. Hence the shim.
  2. **`.claude/settings.json` is picked up live** — file watcher applies hook config to the LIVE fleet immediately, without restart. An edit = live-effect (can verify, can break).
  3. **"Tree dirty between commits" — NORM, not a bug.** Standard state now: `docs/prompts/2-*.md` + `docs/lab/events/<today>.jsonl` (the latter grows on each hook; event `vcs.commit` is written AFTER the commit → the log must be dirty between commits). Swept at the ledger boundary, like queue.jsonl. **Mechanic/finalizer to know** — do not "fix" the non-existent. Rule "porcelain=1 after ledger" from handoff 22 OUTDATED.
  4. **`docs/lab/events/*.jsonl` is committed** (owner-sovereign in PRD + blueprint + README Phase 3). `.gitattributes merge=union` removes merge conflicts. Only `docs/lab/scratch/` is gitignored.
